\displaystyle A(q_{1},q_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{U}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{A(q_{2},P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)+A(q_{1},P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)+A(q_{1},q_{2})}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

(64)

\displaystyle+\frac{U}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{A(k,P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)+A(k,q_{2})+A(k,q_{1})}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{[\cos{k}+\cos{q_{1}}]\,C(q_{2},P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)+[\sin{k}+\sin{q_{1}}]\,S(q_{2},P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{[\cos{k}+\cos{q_{2}}]\,C(q_{1},P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)+[\sin{k}+\sin{q_{2}}]\,S(q_{1},P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{[\cos{k}+\cos{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}]\,C(q_{1},q_{2})+[\sin{k}+\sin{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}]\,S(q_{1},q_{2})}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{[\cos{q_{1}}+\cos{q_{2}}]\,C(k,P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)+[\sin{q_{1}}+\sin{q_{2}}]\,S(k,P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{[\cos{q_{1}}+\cos{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}]\,C(k,q_{2})+[\sin{q_{1}}+\sin{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}]\,S(k,q_{2})}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{[\cos{q_{2}}+\cos{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}]\,C(k,q_{1})+[\sin{q_{2}}+\sin{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}]\,S(k,q_{1})}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}\>.

\displaystyle C(q_{1},q_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{U}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{\cos{k}\,A(q_{2},P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)+\cos{k}\,A(q_{1},P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)+\cos{k}\,A(q_{1},q_{2})}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

(65)

\displaystyle+\frac{U}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{\cos{k}\,A(k,P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)+\cos{k}\,A(k,q_{2})+\cos{k}\,A(k,q_{1})}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{\cos{k}\,[\cos{k}+\cos{q_{1}}]\,C(q_{2},P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)+\cos{k}\,[\sin{k}+\sin{q_{1}}]\,S(q_{2},P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{\cos{k}\,[\cos{k}+\cos{q_{2}}]\,C(q_{1},P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)+\cos{k}\,[\sin{k}+\sin{q_{2}}]\,S(q_{1},P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{\cos{k}\,[\cos{k}+\cos{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}]\,C(q_{1},q_{2})+\cos{k}\,[\sin{k}+\sin{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}]\,S(q_{1},q_{2})}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{\cos{k}\,[\cos{q_{1}}+\cos{q_{2}}]\,C(k,P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)+\cos{k}\,[\sin{q_{1}}+\sin{q_{2}}]\,S(k,P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{\cos{k}\,[\cos{q_{1}}+\cos{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}]\,C(k,q_{2})+\cos{k}\,[\sin{q_{1}}+\sin{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}]\,S(k,q_{2})}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{\cos{k}\,[\cos{q_{2}}+\cos{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}]\,C(k,q_{1})+\cos{k}\,[\sin{q_{2}}+\sin{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}]\,S(k,q_{1})}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}\>.\hskip 14.22636pt

\displaystyle S(q_{1},q_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{U}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{\sin{k}\,A(q_{2},P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)+\sin{k}\,A(q_{1},P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)+\sin{k}\,A(q_{1},q_{2})}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

(66)

\displaystyle+\frac{U}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{\sin{k}\,A(k,P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)+\sin{k}\,A(k,q_{2})+\sin{k}\,A(k,q_{1})}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{\sin{k}\,[\cos{k}+\cos{q_{1}}]\,C(q_{2},P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)+\sin{k}\,[\sin{k}+\sin{q_{1}}]\,S(q_{2},P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{\sin{k}\,[\cos{k}+\cos{q_{2}}]\,C(q_{1},P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)+\sin{k}\,[\sin{k}+\sin{q_{2}}]\,S(q_{1},P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{\sin{k}\,[\cos{k}+\cos{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}]\,C(q_{1},q_{2})+\sin{k}\,[\sin{k}+\sin{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}]\,S(q_{1},q_{2})}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{\cos{k}\,[\cos{q_{1}}+\cos{q_{2}}]\,C(k,P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)+\cos{k}\,[\sin{q_{1}}+\sin{q_{2}}]\,S(k,P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{\sin{k}\,[\cos{q_{1}}+\cos{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}]\,C(k,q_{2})+\sin{k}\,[\sin{q_{1}}+\sin{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}]\,S(k,q_{2})}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{\sin{k}\,[\cos{q_{2}}+\cos{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}]\,C(k,q_{1})+\sin{k}\,[\sin{q_{2}}+\sin{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}]\,S(k,q_{1})}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}\>.\hskip 14.22636pt

G.3
Four
s=0
bosons in 2D

Based on the form of Eq. (
21
) after summation over
{\bf b}=\pm{\bf x},\pm{\bf y}
, we define 5 auxiliary functions:

\displaystyle A({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\hskip 31.2982pt\Psi_{s}({\bf k},{\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})\>,

(67)

\displaystyle C_{x}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\cos{(q_{x})}\Psi_{s}({\bf k},{\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})\>,

(68)

\displaystyle S_{x}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\sin{(q_{x})}\Psi_{s}({\bf k},{\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})\>,

(69)

\displaystyle C_{y}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\cos{(q_{y})}\Psi_{s}({\bf k},{\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})\>,

(70)

\displaystyle S_{y}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\sin{(q_{y})}\Psi_{s}({\bf k},{\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})\>.

(71)

The four-boson wave function is expressed from the Schrödinger equation, Eq. (
21
):

\displaystyle\Psi_{s}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2},{\bf q}_{3},{\bf q}_{4})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle U\>\frac{A({\bf q}_{3},{\bf q}_{4})+A({\bf q}_{2},{\bf q}_{4})+A({\bf q}_{2},{\bf q}_{3})+A({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{4})+A({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{3})+A({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{3})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{4})}

(72)

\displaystyle-V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{1x}}+\cos{q_{2x}})\,C_{x}({\bf q}_{3},{\bf q}_{4})+(\sin{q_{1x}}+\sin{q_{2x}})\,S_{x}({\bf q}_{3},{\bf q}_{4})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{3})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{4})}

\displaystyle-V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{1y}}+\cos{q_{2y}})\,C_{y}({\bf q}_{3},{\bf q}_{4})+(\sin{q_{1y}}+\sin{q_{2y}})\,S_{y}({\bf q}_{3},{\bf q}_{4})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{3})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{4})}

\displaystyle-V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{1x}}+\cos{q_{3x}})\,C_{x}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf q}_{4})+(\sin{q_{1x}}+\sin{q_{3x}})\,S_{x}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf q}_{4})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{3})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{4})}

\displaystyle-V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{1y}}+\cos{q_{3y}})\,C_{y}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf q}_{4})+(\sin{q_{1y}}+\sin{q_{3y}})\,S_{y}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf q}_{4})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{3})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{4})}

\displaystyle-V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{1x}}+\cos{q_{4x}})\,C_{x}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf q}_{3})+(\sin{q_{1x}}+\sin{q_{4x}})\,S_{x}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf q}_{3})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{3})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{4})}

\displaystyle-V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{1y}}+\cos{q_{4y}})\,C_{y}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf q}_{3})+(\sin{q_{1y}}+\sin{q_{4y}})\,S_{y}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf q}_{3})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{3})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{4})}

\displaystyle-V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{2x}}+\cos{q_{3x}})\,C_{x}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{4})+(\sin{q_{2x}}+\sin{q_{3x}})\,S_{x}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{4})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{3})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{4})}

\displaystyle-V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{2y}}+\cos{q_{3y}})\,C_{y}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{4})+(\sin{q_{2y}}+\sin{q_{3y}})\,S_{y}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{4})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{3})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{4})}

\displaystyle-V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{2x}}+\cos{q_{4x}})\,C_{x}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{3})+(\sin{q_{2x}}+\sin{q_{4x}})\,S_{x}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{3})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{3})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{4})}

\displaystyle-V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{2y}}+\cos{q_{4y}})\,C_{y}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{3})+(\sin{q_{2y}}+\sin{q_{4y}})\,S_{y}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{3})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{3})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{4})}

\displaystyle-V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{3x}}+\cos{q_{4x}})\,C_{x}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})+(\sin{q_{3x}}+\sin{q_{4x}})\,S_{x}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{3})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{4})}

\displaystyle-V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{3y}}+\cos{q_{4y}})\,C_{y}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})+(\sin{q_{3y}}+\sin{q_{4y}})\,S_{y}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{3})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{4})}\>.

Substitution of Eq. (
72
) in Eq. (
67
) yields the first of 5 integral equations

\displaystyle A({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{U}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,A({\bf q}_{2},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})+f({\bf k})\,A({\bf q}_{1},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})+f({\bf k})\,A({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

(73)

\displaystyle+\frac{U}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,A({\bf k},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})+f({\bf k})\,A({\bf k},{\bf q}_{2})+f({\bf k})\,A({\bf k},{\bf q}_{1})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\cos{k_{x}}+\cos{q_{1x}}]C_{x}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})+f({\bf k})\,[\sin{k_{x}}+\sin{q_{1x}}]S_{x}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\cos{k_{y}}+\cos{q_{1y}}]C_{y}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})+f({\bf k})\,[\sin{k_{y}}+\sin{q_{1y}}]S_{y}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\cos{k_{x}}+\cos{q_{2x}}]C_{x}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})+f({\bf k})\,[\sin{k_{x}}+\sin{q_{2x}}]S_{x}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\cos{k_{y}}+\cos{q_{2y}}]C_{y}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})+f({\bf k})\,[\sin{k_{y}}+\sin{q_{2y}}]S_{y}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\cos{k_{x}}+\cos{(P_{x}-q_{1x}-q_{2x}-k_{x})}]C_{x}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\sin{k_{x}}+\sin{(P_{x}-q_{1x}-q_{2x}-k_{x})}]S_{x}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\cos{k_{y}}+\cos{(P_{y}-q_{1y}-q_{2y}-k_{y})}]C_{y}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\sin{k_{y}}+\sin{(P_{y}-q_{1y}-q_{2y}-k_{y})}]S_{y}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\cos{q_{1x}}+\cos{q_{2x}}]C_{x}({\bf k},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})+f({\bf k})\,[\sin{q_{1x}}+\sin{q_{2x}}]S_{x}({\bf k},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\cos{q_{1y}}+\cos{q_{2y}}]C_{y}({\bf k},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})+f({\bf k})\,[\sin{q_{1y}}+\sin{q_{2y}}]S_{y}({\bf k},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\cos{q_{1x}}+\cos{(P_{x}-q_{1x}-q_{2x}-k_{x})}]C_{x}({\bf k},{\bf q}_{2})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\sin{q_{1x}}+\sin{(P_{x}-q_{1x}-q_{2x}-k_{x})}]S_{x}({\bf k},{\bf q}_{2})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\cos{q_{1y}}+\cos{(P_{y}-q_{1y}-q_{2y}-k_{y})}]C_{y}({\bf k},{\bf q}_{2})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\sin{q_{1y}}+\sin{(P_{y}-q_{1y}-q_{2y}-k_{y})}]S_{y}({\bf k},{\bf q}_{2})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\cos{q_{2x}}+\cos{(P_{x}-q_{1x}-q_{2x}-k_{x})}]C_{x}({\bf k},{\bf q}_{1})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\sin{q_{2x}}+\sin{(P_{x}-q_{1x}-q_{2x}-k_{x})}]S_{x}({\bf k},{\bf q}_{1})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\cos{q_{2y}}+\cos{(P_{y}-q_{1y}-q_{2y}-k_{y})}]C_{y}({\bf k},{\bf q}_{1})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\sin{q_{2y}}+\sin{(P_{y}-q_{1y}-q_{2y}-k_{y})}]S_{y}({\bf k},{\bf q}_{1})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}\>,

with
f({\bf k})\equiv 1
. The equations for
C_{x}
,
S_{x}
,
C_{y}
, and
S_{y}
are also described by Eq. (
73
) but with
f({\bf k})=\cos{(k_{x})}
,
\sin{(k_{x})}
,
\cos{(k_{y})}
, and
\sin{(k_{y})}
, respectively.

G.4
Four
s=\frac{1}{2}
fermions in 1D.
S=2

Starting with Eq. (
30
), we first sum over
{\bf b}=\pm{\bf x}
to get a common factor of 2. Then we permute the arguments to bring the wave function to a common form
\Psi_{2}(k,q,q,q+q-k)
, and expand the cosines. Next, utilizing conservation of total momentum, we define 2 auxiliary functions

\displaystyle C(q_{1},q_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{k}\cos{(k)}\Psi_{2}(k,q_{1},q_{2},P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)\>,

(74)

\displaystyle S(q_{1},q_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{k}\sin{(k)}\Psi_{2}(k,q_{1},q_{2},P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)\>.

(75)

Both functions are antisymmetric:
C(q_{2},q_{1})=-C(q_{1},q_{2})
and
S(q_{2},q_{1})=-S(q_{1},q_{2})
. The wave function follows from Eq. (
30
)

\displaystyle\Psi_{2}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3},q_{4})=\hskip 5.69046pt

\displaystyle-\>V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{1}}-\cos{q_{2}})\,C(q_{3},q_{4})+(\sin{q_{1}}-\sin{q_{2}})\,S(q_{3},q_{4})}{E-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(q_{3})-\varepsilon(q_{4})}

(76)

\displaystyle-\>V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{3}}-\cos{q_{1}})\,C(q_{2},q_{4})+(\sin{q_{3}}-\sin{q_{1}})\,S(q_{2},q_{4})}{E-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(q_{3})-\varepsilon(q_{4})}

\displaystyle-\>V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{1}}-\cos{q_{4}})\,C(q_{2},q_{3})+(\sin{q_{1}}-\sin{q_{4}})\,S(q_{2},q_{3})}{E-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(q_{3})-\varepsilon(q_{4})}

\displaystyle-\>V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{2}}-\cos{q_{3}})\,C(q_{1},q_{4})+(\sin{q_{2}}-\sin{q_{3}})\,S(q_{1},q_{4})}{E-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(q_{3})-\varepsilon(q_{4})}

\displaystyle-\>V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{4}}-\cos{q_{2}})\,C(q_{1},q_{3})+(\sin{q_{4}}-\sin{q_{2}})\,S(q_{1},q_{3})}{E-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(q_{3})-\varepsilon(q_{4})}

\displaystyle-\>V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{3}}-\cos{q_{4}})\,C(q_{1},q_{2})+(\sin{q_{3}}-\sin{q_{4}})\,S(q_{1},q_{2})}{E-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(q_{3})-\varepsilon(q_{4})}\>.

Substitution of Eq. (
76
) in Eqs. (
74
) and (
75
) yields two coupled integral equations

\displaystyle C(q_{1},q_{2})=\hskip 5.69046pt

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{\cos{k}\,[\cos{k}-\cos{q_{1}}]\,C(q_{2},P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)+\cos{k}\,[\sin{k}-\sin{q_{1}}]\,S(q_{2},P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

(77)

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{\cos{k}\,[\cos{q_{2}}-\cos{k}]\,C(q_{1},P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)+\cos{k}\,[\sin{q_{2}}-\sin{k}]\,S(q_{1},P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{\cos{k}\,[\cos{k}-\cos{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}]\,C(q_{1},q_{2})+\cos{k}\,[\sin{k}-\sin{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}]\,S(q_{1},q_{2})}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{\cos{k}\,[\cos{q_{1}}-\cos{q_{2}}]\,C(k,P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)+\cos{k}\,[\sin{q_{1}}-\sin{q_{2}}]\,S(k,P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{\cos{k}\,[\cos{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}-\cos{q_{1}}]\,C(k,q_{2})+\cos{k}\,[\sin{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}-\sin{q_{1}}]\,S(k,q_{2})}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{\cos{k}\,[\cos{q_{2}}-\cos{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}]\,C(k,q_{1})+\cos{k}\,[\sin{q_{2}}-\sin{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}]\,S(k,q_{1})}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}\>,\hskip 14.22636pt

\displaystyle S(q_{1},q_{2})=\hskip 5.69046pt

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{\sin{k}\,[\cos{k}-\cos{q_{1}}]\,C(q_{2},P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)+\sin{k}\,[\sin{k}-\sin{q_{1}}]\,S(q_{2},P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

(78)

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{\sin{k}\,[\cos{q_{2}}-\cos{k}]\,C(q_{1},P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)+\sin{k}\,[\sin{q_{2}}-\sin{k}]\,S(q_{1},P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{\sin{k}\,[\cos{k}-\cos{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}]\,C(q_{1},q_{2})+\sin{k}\,[\sin{k}-\sin{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}]\,S(q_{1},q_{2})}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{\sin{k}\,[\cos{q_{1}}-\cos{q_{2}}]\,C(k,P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)+\sin{k}\,[\sin{q_{1}}-\sin{q_{2}}]\,S(k,P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{\sin{k}\,[\cos{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}-\cos{q_{1}}]\,C(k,q_{2})+\sin{k}\,[\sin{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}-\sin{q_{1}}]\,S(k,q_{2})}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{k}\frac{\sin{k}\,[\cos{q_{2}}-\cos{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}]\,C(k,q_{1})+\sin{k}\,[\sin{q_{2}}-\sin{(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}]\,S(k,q_{1})}{E-\varepsilon(k)-\varepsilon(q_{1})-\varepsilon(q_{2})-\varepsilon(P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)}\>.\hskip 14.22636pt

G.5
Four
s=\frac{1}{2}
fermions in 2D.
S=2

Starting with Eq. (
30
), we sum over
{\bf b}=\pm{\bf x},\pm{\bf y}
, permute the arguments to bring the wave function to a common form
\Psi_{2}({\bf k},{\bf q},{\bf q},{\bf q}+{\bf q}-{\bf k})
, and expand the cosines. Utilizing conservation of total momentum, we define 4 auxiliary functions

\displaystyle C_{x}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\cos{(k_{x})}\Psi_{2}({\bf k},{\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})\>,

(79)

\displaystyle S_{x}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\sin{(k_{x})}\Psi_{2}({\bf k},{\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})\>,

(80)

\displaystyle C_{y}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\cos{(k_{y})}\Psi_{2}({\bf k},{\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})\>,

(81)

\displaystyle S_{y}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\sin{(k_{y})}\Psi_{2}({\bf k},{\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})\>.

(82)

All four functions are antisymmetric. For example,
S_{x}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf q}_{1})=-S_{x}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})
and so on. The wave function follows from Eq. (
30
)

\displaystyle\Psi_{2}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2},{\bf q}_{3},{\bf q}_{4})=\hskip 5.69046pt

\displaystyle-V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{1x}}-\cos{q_{2x}})\,C_{x}({\bf q}_{3},{\bf q}_{4})+(\sin{q_{1x}}-\sin{q_{2x}})\,S_{x}({\bf q}_{3},{\bf q}_{4})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{3})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{4})}

(83)

\displaystyle-V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{1y}}-\cos{q_{2y}})\,C_{y}({\bf q}_{3},{\bf q}_{4})+(\sin{q_{1y}}-\sin{q_{2y}})\,S_{y}({\bf q}_{3},{\bf q}_{4})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{3})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{4})}

\displaystyle-V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{3x}}-\cos{q_{1x}})\,C_{x}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf q}_{4})+(\sin{q_{3x}}-\sin{q_{1x}})\,S_{x}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf q}_{4})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{3})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{4})}

\displaystyle-V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{3y}}-\cos{q_{1y}})\,C_{y}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf q}_{4})+(\sin{q_{3y}}-\sin{q_{1y}})\,S_{y}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf q}_{4})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{3})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{4})}

\displaystyle-V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{1x}}-\cos{q_{4x}})\,C_{x}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf q}_{3})+(\sin{q_{1x}}-\sin{q_{4x}})\,S_{x}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf q}_{3})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{3})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{4})}

\displaystyle-V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{1y}}-\cos{q_{4y}})\,C_{y}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf q}_{3})+(\sin{q_{1y}}-\sin{q_{4y}})\,S_{y}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf q}_{3})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{3})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{4})}

\displaystyle-V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{2x}}-\cos{q_{3x}})\,C_{x}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{4})+(\sin{q_{2x}}-\sin{q_{3x}})\,S_{x}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{4})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{3})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{4})}

\displaystyle-V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{2y}}-\cos{q_{3y}})\,C_{y}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{4})+(\sin{q_{2y}}-\sin{q_{3y}})\,S_{y}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{4})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{3})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{4})}

\displaystyle-V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{4x}}-\cos{q_{2x}})\,C_{x}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{3})+(\sin{q_{4x}}-\sin{q_{2x}})\,S_{x}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{3})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{3})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{4})}

\displaystyle-V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{4y}}-\cos{q_{2y}})\,C_{y}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{3})+(\sin{q_{4y}}-\sin{q_{2y}})\,S_{y}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{3})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{3})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{4})}

\displaystyle-V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{3x}}-\cos{q_{4x}})\,C_{x}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})+(\sin{q_{3x}}-\sin{q_{4x}})\,S_{x}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{3})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{4})}

\displaystyle-V\>\frac{(\cos{q_{3y}}-\cos{q_{4y}})\,C_{y}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})+(\sin{q_{3y}}-\sin{q_{4y}})\,S_{y}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{3})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{4})}\>.

) in Eq. (
79
) yields the first of 4 integral equations

\displaystyle C_{x}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})=\hskip 5.69046pt

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\cos{k_{x}}-\cos{q_{1x}}]C_{x}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})+f({\bf k})\,[\sin{k_{x}}-\sin{q_{1x}}]S_{x}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

(84)

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\cos{k_{y}}-\cos{q_{1y}}]C_{y}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})+f({\bf k})\,[\sin{k_{y}}-\sin{q_{1y}}]S_{y}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\cos{q_{2x}}-\cos{k_{x}}]C_{x}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})+f({\bf k})\,[\sin{q_{2x}}-\sin{k_{x}}]S_{x}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\cos{k_{x}}-\cos{(P_{x}-q_{1x}-q_{2x}-k_{x})}]C_{x}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\sin{k_{x}}-\sin{(P_{x}-q_{1x}-q_{2x}-k_{x})}]S_{x}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\sin{k_{y}}-\sin{(P_{y}-q_{1y}-q_{2y}-k_{y})}]S_{y}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\cos{q_{1x}}-\cos{q_{2x}}]C_{x}({\bf k},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})+f({\bf k})\,[\sin{q_{1x}}-\sin{q_{2x}}]S_{x}({\bf k},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\cos{(P_{y}-q_{1y}-q_{2y}-k_{y})}-\cos{q_{1y}}]C_{y}({\bf k},{\bf q}_{2})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\cos{q_{2x}}-\cos{(P_{x}-q_{1x}-q_{2x}-k_{x})}]C_{x}({\bf k},{\bf q}_{1})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\sin{q_{2x}}-\sin{(P_{x}-q_{1x}-q_{2x}-k_{x})}]S_{x}({\bf k},{\bf q}_{1})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\cos{q_{2y}}-\cos{(P_{y}-q_{1y}-q_{2y}-k_{y})}]C_{y}({\bf k},{\bf q}_{1})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\sin{q_{2y}}-\sin{(P_{y}-q_{1y}-q_{2y}-k_{y})}]S_{y}({\bf k},{\bf q}_{1})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}\>,

with

. The equations for

Summation over

in Eq. (

. The resulting Schrödinger equation requires 5 auxiliary functions

\displaystyle A_{14}(q_{1},q_{2})

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{k}\hskip 22.76228pt\Psi_{1}(k,q_{1},q_{2};P-q_{1}-q_{2}-k)\>;\hskip 28.45274ptA_{14}(q_{2},q_{1})=-A_{14}(q_{1},q_{2})\>,

\displaystyle-2V\>\frac{\cos{q_{1x}}\,C_{x14}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf q}_{3})+\sin{q_{1x}}\,S_{x14}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf q}_{3})+\cos{q_{1y}}\,C_{y14}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf q}_{3})+\sin{q_{1y}}\,S_{y14}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf q}_{3})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{3})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{4})}

\displaystyle A_{13}(q_{1},q_{2})

\displaystyle\hskip 2.84544pt-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\sin{(P_{x}-q_{1x}-q_{2x}-k_{x})}-\sin{q_{2x}}]\,S_{x34}({\bf k},{\bf q}_{1})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle\hskip 2.84544pt-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\cos{(P_{y}-q_{1y}-q_{2y}-k_{y})}-\cos{q_{2y}}]\,C_{y34}({\bf k},{\bf q}_{1})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle\hskip 2.84544pt-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\sin{(P_{y}-q_{1y}-q_{2y}-k_{y})}-\sin{q_{2y}}]\,S_{y34}({\bf k},{\bf q}_{1})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}\>,

where
f({\bf k})\equiv 1
. The equations for
C_{x13}
,
S_{x13}
,
C_{y13}
, and
S_{y13}
have the same right-hand-side as Eq. (
134
) but with
f({\bf k})=\cos{k_{x}}
,
f({\bf k})=\sin{k_{x}}
,
f({\bf k})=\cos{k_{y}}
, and
f({\bf k})=\sin{k_{y}}
, respectively.

The third group includes the equations for
C_{x34}
,
S_{x34}
,
C_{y34}
, and
S_{y34}
. The equation for
C_{x34}
reads

\displaystyle C_{x34}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})=\hskip 5.69046pt

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\frac{U}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,A_{13}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})-f({\bf k})\,A_{13}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf k})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

(135)

\displaystyle\hskip 2.84544pt+\frac{U}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{-f({\bf k})\,A_{13}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})+f({\bf k})\,A_{13}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf k})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle\hskip 2.84544pt-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,(\cos{q_{1x}}-\cos{q_{2x}})\,C_{x12}({\bf k},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle\hskip 2.84544pt-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,(\sin{q_{1x}}-\sin{q_{2x}})\,S_{x12}({\bf k},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle\hskip 2.84544pt-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,(\cos{q_{1y}}-\cos{q_{2y}})\,C_{y12}({\bf k},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle\hskip 2.84544pt-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,(\sin{q_{1y}}-\sin{q_{2y}})\,S_{y12}({\bf k},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{2V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,\cos{q_{1x}}\,C_{x13}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})+f({\bf k})\,\sin{q_{1x}}\,S_{x13}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{2V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,\cos{q_{1y}}\,C_{y13}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})+f({\bf k})\,\sin{q_{1y}}\,S_{y13}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{2V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{-f({\bf k})\,\cos{q_{1x}}\,C_{x13}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf k})-f({\bf k})\,\sin{q_{1x}}\,S_{x13}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf k})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{2V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{-f({\bf k})\,\cos{q_{1y}}\,C_{y13}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf k})-f({\bf k})\,\sin{q_{1y}}\,S_{y13}({\bf q}_{2},{\bf k})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{2V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{-f({\bf k})\,\cos{q_{2x}}\,C_{x13}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})-f({\bf k})\,\sin{q_{2x}}\,S_{x13}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{2V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{-f({\bf k})\,\cos{q_{2y}}\,C_{y13}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})-f({\bf k})\,\sin{q_{2y}}\,S_{y13}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle-\frac{2V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,\cos{q_{2x}}\,C_{x13}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf k})+f({\bf k})\,\sin{q_{2x}}\,S_{x13}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf k})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle\hskip 2.84544pt-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\cos{k_{x}}-\cos{(P_{x}-q_{1x}-q_{2x}-k_{x})}]\,C_{x34}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle\hskip 2.84544pt-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\sin{k_{x}}-\sin{(P_{x}-q_{1x}-q_{2x}-k_{x})}]\,S_{x34}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle\hskip 2.84544pt-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\cos{k_{y}}-\cos{(P_{y}-q_{1y}-q_{2y}-k_{y})}]\,C_{y34}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}

\displaystyle\hskip 2.84544pt-\frac{V}{N}\sum_{\bf k}\frac{f({\bf k})\,[\sin{k_{y}}-\sin{(P_{y}-q_{1y}-q_{2y}-k_{y})}]\,S_{y34}({\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2})}{E-\varepsilon({\bf k})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{1})-\varepsilon({\bf q}_{2})-\varepsilon({\bf P}-{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}-{\bf k})}\>,
